# Commands · F · part 2

1,468 entries, fg-feature to fix-git-issues. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fg-feature**: Scaffold or extend a feature — route tree, data-access, state slice, type-only models, ui/ surfaces, and the required FEATURE.md — per ARCHITECTURE.md §8.3/§8.4. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-web](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-feature.md) · ★1)
- **fg-migrate**: Generate, apply, or review a Doctrine migration on the correct database — this app has two entity managers with separate migration histories. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-api](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-migrate.md) · ★1)
- **fg-module**: Scaffold a brand-new bounded context under src/&lt;Module&gt; — the four-layer skeleton, first use case, ports, wiring, Doctrine mapping, security rules, MODULE.md, and baseline tests. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-api](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-module.md) · ★1)
- **fg-pipe**: Create an Angular pipe at shared/&lt;concept&gt;/ui/pipes/&lt;name&gt;/ — the repo has zero pipes today, so this sets the precedent and must update ARCHITECTURE.md §9.2 in the same change. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-web](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-pipe.md) · ★1)
- **fg-port**: Add a port and its adapter — the Application/Port interface, the Infrastructure implementation, the config alias, the entity-manager wiring, and the test. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-api](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-port.md) · ★1)
- **fg-quality**: Run the backend quality gate — cs-fix, phpstan, deptrac, container/yaml lint, and tests — stopping at the first failure. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-api](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-quality.md) · ★1)
- **fg-quality**: Run the FireGuard Web quality gate — oxfmt, oxlint, targeted or full tests, and the strict Angular build — stopping at the first failure. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-web](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-quality.md) · ★1)
- **fg-security-review**: Security-review changes touching auth, OAuth2/OIDC, sessions, trusted devices, OTP/MFA, RBAC, the audit ledger, tenant scoping, secrets, or the Stripe webhook. Read-only. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-api](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-security-review.md) · ★1)
- **fg-service**: Create a service — transport (extends HydraApiService), behavioral, access helper, or a pure data adapter — routed to the right kind and folder per ARCHITECTURE.md §10.6–§10.8. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-web](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-service.md) · ★1)
- **fg-spartan**: Build or adjust a spartan/ui surface — table, form, dialog, sheet, menu — with Tailwind v4 and the semantic theme tokens, checking the catalog before hand-rolling anything. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-web](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-spartan.md) · ★1)
- **fg-store**: Create or refactor an NgRx SignalStore slice — CallState vs withQueryState vs withEntities, rxMethod + tapResponse, typed events, scoping, and the SSR handoff — per ARCHITECTURE.md §10.11. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-web](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-store.md) · ★1)
- **fg-test**: Author or repair unit/integration specs at the boundary each unit owns — stores, data-access services, guards/resolvers, pages, presentational components — run via npx ng test, never bare vitest. ([DevSkyLex/fireguard-web](https://github.com/DevSkyLex/fireguard-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fg-test.md) · ★1)
